The recipient is therefore both gifted and practically responsive.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000323/B002","root_000552/B001","root_001583/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_4dbdb66c562d73b7092e","text":"1. **Guidance as a gently delivered benefit.** **ه د ي B001** gives gentle guidance toward a way; **B004** gives a gracious gift sent to someone close; **ر ح م B001** gives mercy; **ح س ن B002** gives beneficent or skillful action. The coordinated indefinites make guidance and mercy parallel provisions for those whose action benefits others. I infer reciprocity without making it a transaction: practiced beneficence disposes one to receive direction as a gift. The line reads as relational delivery, not merely abstract information.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000552/B001","root_001583/B001","root_001583/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_87c6187a195588d011b9","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"31:3:1\",\"root_001583\",[\"B001\",\"B004\"]],[\"31:3:2\",\"root_000552\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Doğru yol, yalnızca bilgi değil, merhametle yakına ulaştırılan bir armağan gibi işler.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001583/B010"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_9cbad694bd383275ca16","text":"Ayat 31:18–19 made **ه د ي B010** unexpectedly bodily through calibrated gait and voice, and 31:22 returned *muḥsin* as the active state accompanying surrendered orientation. Guidance and beneficence became visible comportment rather than abstract labels.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001583/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_a6ed24baed8fc850a131","text":"2. **A direction made legible through beneficent action.** Lexical evidence: `ه د ي B002` covers course, manner, and intended direction, and `ح س ن B002` covers skillful, beneficent action. Structural cue: `لِّلْمُحْسِنِينَ` assigns the guidance-mercy pair to agents whose worship and giving are specified in 31:4 and whose faith and sound deeds recur in 31:8. Assigned role: beneficent action is the embodied bearing of the directed course. Abductive move (inferred): practice does not cause the gift, but makes its direction visible as a lived trajectory. Reading change: `لِّلْمُحْسِنِينَ` identifies readers who enact guidance, not merely a favored audience receiving it passively.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000552/B001","root_001583/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_c95300e9b092926cc3c0","text":"1. **Guidance as a graciously delivered gift.** Lexical evidence: alongside ordinary direction in `ه د ي B001`, `ه د ي B004` is a gracious gift sent to someone close; `ر ح م B001` supplies beneficent tenderness. Structural cue: guidance and mercy are coordinated as two indefinite gifts, 31:4 uses the granting verb `يُؤْتُونَ`, and 31:5 locates guidance as coming from their Lord. Assigned role: the gift branch makes guidance a transferred benefit rather than bare information. Abductive move (inferred): the adjacency of mercy and the local giving language activates the gift image without replacing the directional sense. Reading change: the ayah presents orientation itself as a merciful bestowal that establishes closeness and obligation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000323/B001","root_001583/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_e30a207efd6570a24e94","text":"2. **Guidance as composed bearing.** **ه د ي B002** (course and intended direction) and **B010** (calm stillness and composed bearing) are activated by the later instructions governing gait and voice; **ح س ن B001** supplies beauty opposed to ugliness. The hidden move is that an inwardly intended course becomes visible in bodily manner. Thus the ayah can announce a mercy that shapes comportment, while still retaining guidance as truth-direction.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
